### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

package main

import (

)

func statusHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")

}

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### User: Write a Python function that validates an email with a simple regex.
### Assistant: ```python
import re

_EMAIL = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$')

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_EMAIL.match(value or ''))
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

bot = commands.Bot(command_prefix='!', intents=discord.Intents.default())

@bot.command()
async def ping(ctx):
    await ctx.send('Pong')

bot.run('TOKEN')
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### Assistant: ```python
import re

_EMAIL = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$')

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

    root.mainloop()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write SQL to select the top 5 customers by total order amount.
### Assistant: ```sql
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, SUM(o.amount) AS total_amount
FROM customers c
JOIN orders o ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC
LIMIT 5;
```

### User: Write a Go HTTP handler that returns JSON status ok.
### Assistant: ```go
package main

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "net/http"
)

func statusHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    _ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"status": "ok"})
}
```

class GreetingIn(BaseModel):

def greet(payload: GreetingIn):
    return {'message': f'Hello, {payload.name}!'}
```

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

### User: Write SQL to select the top 5 customers by total order amount.
### Assistant: ```sql
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, SUM(o.amount) AS total_amount
FROM customers c
JOIN orders o ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC
LIMIT 5;
```

)

    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")

}

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

    root.mainloop()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];

  return out;
}

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):

### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

}

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

### User: Write SQL to select the top 5 customers by total order amount.
### Assistant: ```sql
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, SUM(o.amount) AS total_amount
FROM customers c
JOIN orders o ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC
LIMIT 5;
```

### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### User: Write a Python function that validates an email with a simple regex.
### Assistant: ```python
import re

_EMAIL = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$')

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_EMAIL.match(value or ''))
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

if __name__ == '__main__':

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

### User: Write SQL to select the top 5 customers by total order amount.
### Assistant: ```sql
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, SUM(o.amount) AS total_amount
FROM customers c
JOIN orders o ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC
LIMIT 5;
```

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### Assistant: ```sql
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, SUM(o.amount) AS total_amount
FROM customers c
JOIN orders o ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC
LIMIT 5;
```

package main

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "net/http"
)

func statusHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    _ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"status": "ok"})
}
```

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

import re

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

    root.mainloop()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

    root.mainloop()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### Assistant: ```python
import re

_EMAIL = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$')

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_EMAIL.match(value or ''))
```

### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

bot = commands.Bot(command_prefix='!', intents=discord.Intents.default())

@bot.command()
async def ping(ctx):
    await ctx.send('Pong')

bot.run('TOKEN')
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### Assistant: ```python
import re

_EMAIL = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}$')

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

    root.mainloop()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write SQL to select the top 5 customers by total order amount.
### Assistant: ```sql
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, SUM(o.amount) AS total_amount
FROM customers c
JOIN orders o ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC
LIMIT 5;
```

### User: Write a Go HTTP handler that returns JSON status ok.
### Assistant: ```go
package main

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "net/http"
)

func statusHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    _ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"status": "ok"})
}
```

class GreetingIn(BaseModel):

def greet(payload: GreetingIn):
    return {'message': f'Hello, {payload.name}!'}
```

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

### User: Write SQL to select the top 5 customers by total order amount.
### Assistant: ```sql
SELECT c.customer_id, c.name, SUM(o.amount) AS total_amount
FROM customers c
JOIN orders o ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id
GROUP BY c.customer_id, c.name
ORDER BY total_amount DESC
LIMIT 5;
```

)

    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")

}

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

def is_valid_email(value: str) -> bool:

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Java method that returns the factorial of n.
### Assistant: ```java
public class MathUtils {
    public static long factorial(int n) {
        if (n < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("n must be >= 0");
        long result = 1L;
        for (int i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
            result *= i;
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that binary-searches a sorted list and returns the index or -1.
### Assistant: ```python
def binary_search(arr, target):
    lo, hi = 0, len(arr) - 1
    while lo <= hi:
        mid = (lo + hi) // 2
        if arr[mid] == target:
            return mid
        if arr[mid] < target:
            lo = mid + 1
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    return -1
```

### User: Write a JavaScript function that debounce-calls fn after wait ms.
### Assistant: ```javascript
function debounce(fn, wait) {
  let timer = null;
  return function (...args) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
  };
}
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python class LRUCache with get and put methods.
### Assistant: ```python
from collections import OrderedDict

class LRUCache:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.data = OrderedDict()

    def get(self, key: int) -> int:
        if key not in self.data:
            return -1
        self.data.move_to_end(key)
        return self.data[key]

    def put(self, key: int, value: int) -> None:
        if key in self.data:
            self.data.move_to_end(key)
        self.data[key] = value
        if len(self.data) > self.capacity:
            self.data.popitem(last=False)
```

    root.mainloop()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a Python async function that fetches a URL with aiohttp and returns text.
### Assistant: ```python
import aiohttp

async def fetch_text(url: str) -> str:
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        async with session.get(url, timeout=20) as resp:
            resp.raise_for_status()
            return await resp.text()
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python function that merges two sorted lists into one sorted list.
### Assistant: ```python
def merge_sorted(a, b):
    i = j = 0
    out = []
    while i < len(a) and j < len(b):
        if a[i] <= b[j]:
            out.append(a[i])
            i += 1
        else:
            out.append(b[j])
            j += 1
    out.extend(a[i:])
    out.extend(b[j:])
    return out
```

### System: You are Navine AI. Write complete runnable code only. No placeholders, no TODO, no explanations.
### User: Write a Python CLI that counts lines, words, and chars in a file path argv[1].
### Assistant: ```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    lines = text.count('\n') + (1 if text and not text.endswith('\n') else 0)
    words = len(text.split())
    chars = len(text)
    print(f'{lines} {words} {chars} {path}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a TypeScript function that typesafely picks keys from an object.
### Assistant: ```typescript
export function pick<T extends object, K extends keyof T>(obj: T, keys: K[]): Pick<T, K> {
  const out = {} as Pick<T, K>;
  for (const key of keys) {
    out[key] = obj[key];
  }
  return out;
}
```

### User: Write a pygame window 640x480 titled Navine that exits on quit.
### Assistant: ```python
import pygame

def main():
    pygame.init()
    screen = pygame.display.set_mode((640, 480))
    pygame.display.set_caption('Navine')
    running = True
    clock = pygame.time.Clock()
    while running:
        for event in pygame.event.get():
            if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
                running = False
        screen.fill((20, 24, 32))
        pygame.display.flip()
        clock.tick(60)
    pygame.quit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

### User: Write a recursive Python directory walker that yields file paths.
### Assistant: ```python
from pathlib import Path

def walk_files(root: str):
    base = Path(root)
    for path in base.rglob('*'):
        if path.is_file():
            yield str(path)
```
